The existing complete-configuration comparison runner now accepts the native affine arithmetic only for a standalone pilot. It requires the distinct native memory-resource identity and refuses a reference-arithmetic receipt, a composite deployment or statistical held-out admission. All sixteen runner tests pass. Its real receipt parser reads both previously excluded native trials without changing their exclusion or treating them as a new comparison. This is a narrow existing-runner compatibility change, not a new evaluation framework.

The prospective comparison fixes three adjacent paired rounds at 14 and 22 GB, using the same short fixed decode, ordinary-context fixed decode and natural coding-answer workloads as the previous practical trial. Both arms preserve the Desktop short-prompt policy, two draft tokens, automatic live management, automatic GPU power policy, prefix reuse and the actual pack-specific draft/lookahead choices. All four CLI plan-only proposals complete without loading a model. The original and smaller pack retain separate physical and numerical identities. Existing process, headroom, pressure, output, staging and cleanup limits remain.

The frozen protocol digest is e60b1010b9727d247990d5a6d3cd72ac364b2ab366db70ae3c5eb0409119faed. At the clean-start check the system reports nominal thermal state and no Low Power Mode, but the unrelated Bluetooth-service process consumes a full CPU core. The outer check refuses before any model process starts. No new performance result exists. Preserve the prepared comparison for a later clean start; do not substitute excluded earlier observations or silently restart the system service. Public permission, actual alternative setup/activation and promotion remain open.
